Please use a DOCX file or a text-based PDF (not scanned/image)"</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">The problem is clear: your RecruitAI export uses <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">html2pdf.js</code> which renders the resume as <strong>images</strong> inside the PDF — so there's no text layer to extract.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">The fix is to <strong>add a DOCX export</strong> to your resume builder. When the user clicks "Export DOCX", it generates a proper Word document with real text — which can then be re-uploaded and parsed perfectly.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Let me check the skill first, then build the solution.</p></div></div><div class="mt-4"><div class="grid grid-rows-[auto_auto] min-w-0"><div class="row-start-1 col-start-1 min-w-0"><div class="min-w-0 pl-2 py-1.5"><div class="flex items-center gap-2"><button class="group/status flex items-center gap-2 py-1 text-sm transition-colors cursor-pointer text-left text-text-500 hover:text-text-300 flex-1 min-w-0" aria-expanded="false"><div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 min-w-0" style="opacity: 1;"><span class="truncate font-base">Check frontend skill</span><span class="inline-flex transition-transform duration-200 shrink-0 -rotate-90"><div style="width: 12px; height: 12px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" style="flex-shrink: 0;"><path d="M14.128 7.165a.502.502 0 0 1 .744.67l-4.5 5-.078.07a.5.5 0 0 1-.666-.07l-4.5-5-.06-.082a.501.501 0 0 1 .729-.656l.075.068L10 11.752z"></path></svg></div></span></div></button></div><span class="sr-only" role="status" aria-live="polite">Check frontend skill</span><div class="grid transition-[grid-template-rows] duration-300 ease-out" style="grid-template-rows: 0fr;"><div class="overflow-hidden min-w-0"></div></div></div></div><div class="row-start-2 col-start-1 relative grid isolate min-w-0"><div class="row-start-1 col-start-1 relative z-[2] min-w-0"><div><div class="standard-markdown grid-cols-1 grid [&amp;_&gt;_*]:min-w-0 gap-3 standard-markdown"><p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">The solution here is purely a JS fix — adding a <strong>docx.js</strong> library to generate a real DOCX from the resume data in the browser. No design changes needed.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Here's exactly what to do. Paste your current <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">resume-builder.html</code> and I'll add:</p>

<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">A <strong>Export DOCX</strong> button next to Export PDF</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">docx.js</code> library in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">&lt;head&gt;</code></li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">A <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">exportDOCX()</code> function that builds a proper Word document from all your resume fields</li>

opacity: 1; transform: none;"><div class="contents"><div data-is-streaming="false" class="group relative relative pb-3" style="opacity: 1; transform: none;"><h2 class="sr-only select-none">Claude responded: The reason is simple — all PDFs exported from a browser (html2pdf, jsPDF, Chrome print, etc.</h2><div class="font-claude-response relative leading-[1.65rem] [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:bg-bg-000/50 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-0.5 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-border-400 [&amp;_.ignore-pre-bg&gt;div]:bg-transparent [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8"><div><div class="standard-markdown grid-cols-1 grid [&amp;_&gt;_*]:min-w-0 gap-3 standard-markdown"><p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">The reason is simple — <strong>all PDFs exported from a browser</strong> (html2pdf, jsPDF, Chrome print, etc.) render HTML as <strong>images</strong> inside the PDF. There is no text layer. So <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">fitz</code> finds 0 characters.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">The only way to fix this is to <strong>not rely on PDF text extraction at all</strong> for RecruitAI-generated resumes. Instead, when the user uploads a PDF that has no extractable text, use <strong>OCR</strong> to read it.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Add this to your <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">extract_text_from_file</code> function in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">app.py</code> as a final fallback:</p>
<div role="group" aria-label="python code" tabindex="0" class="relative group/copy bg-bg-000/50 border-0.5 border-border-400 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent-100"><div class="sticky opacity-0 group-hover/copy:opacity-100 group-focus-within/copy:opacity-100 top-2 py-2 h-12 w-0 float-right"><div class="absolute right-0 h-8 px-2 items-center inline-flex z-10"><button class="inline-flex

</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(129, 136, 152);"># pip install pytesseract pillow --break-system-packages</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(129, 136, 152);"># Also install Tesseract OCR: https://github.com/UB-Mannheim/tesseract/wiki (Windows)</span><span>
</span></span><span>

</span></span><span><span>        </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(204, 123, 244);">from</span><span> PIL </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(204, 123, 244);">import</span><span> Image
</span></span><span><span>        </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(204, 123, 244);">import</span><span> fitz </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(204, 123, 244);">as</span><span> _fitz
</span></span><span><span>        doc2 </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(234, 236, 240);">=</span><span> _fitz</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">.</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(251, 173, 96);">open</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">(</span><span>filepath</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>        ocr_parts </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(234, 236, 240);">=</span><span> </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">[</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">]</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>        </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(204, 123, 244);">for</span><span> page </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(204, 123, 244);">in</span><span> doc2</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">:</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>            pix </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(234, 236, 240);">=</span><span> page</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">.</span><span>get_pixmap</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">(</span><span>dpi</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(234, 236, 240);">=</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(94, 237, 237);">200</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>            img </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(234, 236, 240);">=</span><span> Image</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">.</span><span>frombytes</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">(</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(155, 233, 99);">"RGB"</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">,</span><span> </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">[</span><span>pix</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">.</span><span>width</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">,</span><span> pix</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">.</span><span>height</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">]</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">,</span><span> pix</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">.</span><span>samples</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>            ocr_parts</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">.</span><span>append</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">(</span><span>pytesseract</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">.</span><span>image_to_string</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">(</span><span>img</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">)</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>        text </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(234, 236, 240);">=</span><span> </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(155, 233, 99);">"\n"</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">.</span><span>join</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">(</span><span>ocr_parts</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">)</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>    </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(204, 123, 244);">except</span><span> Exception </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(204, 123, 244);">as</span><span> e</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">:</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>        </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(204, 123, 244);">print</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">(</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(155, 233, 99);">"OCR failed:"</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">,</span><span> e</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">)</span></span></code></pre></div></div>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>BUT</strong> — OCR on Windows requires installing Tesseract separately which is annoying.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Easier alternative</strong> — the real fix is to make your RecruitAI PDF export text-based. Replace <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">html2pdf.js</code> with <strong>jsPDF + html2canvas</strong> in a way that embeds text. But honestly even that doesn't reliably produce text-layer PDFs.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>My recommendation:</strong> Just guide users to use DOCX. Update the upload error message to be clearer:</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Find in your HTML:</p>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Step 1 — Create a <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">.gitignore</code></strong> first (important — don't push secrets):</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Create a file called <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">.gitignore</code> in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">C:\Users\anura\OneDrive\Desktop\RecruitAI\</code> with this content:</p>

font-weight: bold;">#</span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(244, 123, 133); font-weight: bold;"> 🤖 RecruitAI</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span>An AI-powered recruitment platform built with Flask and Groq (LLaMA 3.3), designed to streamline hiring for recruiters and job searching for candidates.
</span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222); font-weight: bold;">##</span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(244, 123, 133); font-weight: bold;"> ✨ Features</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222); font-weight: bold;">###</span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(244, 123, 133); font-weight: bold;"> For Candidates</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> 📄 </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">Resume Builder</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — Build professional resumes with 8+ templates, live preview, and PDF/DOCX export
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> 🧠 </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">AI Resume Parser</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — Upload your existing resume (DOCX) and auto-fill the builder
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> 📊 </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">ATS Score</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — Get instant ATS compatibility score with improvement tips
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> 🚀 </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">AI Career Tools</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — Generate portfolio project ideas and career roadmaps
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> 💼 </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">Job Board</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — Browse and apply to jobs with one click
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222); font-weight: bold;">###</span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(244, 123, 133); font-weight: bold;"> For Recruiters</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> 📋 </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">Job Posting</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — Post and manage job listings
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> 🔍 </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">AI Resume Screening</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — Automatically screen and rank candidates
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> 📊 </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">Analytics Dashboard</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — Track applications, views, and hiring metrics
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> 🤝 </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">Candidate Comparison</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — Compare candidates side by side with AI insights
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> 🎤 </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">AI Interview Questions</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — Generate role-specific interview questions
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222); font-weight: bold;">##</span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(244, 123, 133); font-weight: bold;"> 🛠 Tech Stack</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">Backend</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — Python, Flask, SQLite
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">AI</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — Groq API (LLaMA 3.3 70B)
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">Frontend</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — HTML, CSS, JavaScript
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">PDF Export</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — html2pdf.js
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token list" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">-</span><span> </span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span class="token token bold content">DOCX Export</span><span class="token token bold" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">**</span><span> — docx.js
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222); font-weight: bold;">##</span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(244, 123, 133); font-weight: bold;"> 🚀 Getting Started</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222); font-weight: bold;">###</span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(244, 123, 133); font-weight: bold;"> 1. Clone the repo</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token code" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">```</span><span class="token token code code-language">bash</span><span class="token token code">
</span></span><span><span class="token token code"></span><span class="token token code code-block language-bash" style="color: rgb(112, 184, 255);">git</span><span class="token token code code-block language-bash"> clone https://github.com/Jyoti2103singh/RecruitAI.git
</span></span><span><span class="token token code code-block language-bash"></span><span class="token token code code-block language-bash" style="color: rgb(251, 173, 96);">cd</span><span class="token token code code-block language-bash"> RecruitAI/backend</span><span class="token token code">
</span></span><span><span class="token token code"></span><span class="token token code" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">```</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222); font-weight: bold;">###</span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(244, 123, 133); font-weight: bold;"> 2. Install dependencies</span><span>
</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token code" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">```</span><span class="token token code code-language">bash</span><span class="token token code">
</span></span><span><span class="token token code"></span><span class="token token code code-block language-bash">pip </span><span class="token token code code-block language-bash" style="color: rgb(112, 184, 255);">install</span><span class="token token code code-block language-bash"> -r requirements.txt</span><span class="token token code">
</span></span><span><span class="token token code"></span><span class="token token code" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">```</span><span>
</span></span><span>
</span><span><span></span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222); font-weight: bold;">###</span><span class="token token title" style="color: rgb(244, 123, 133); font-weight: bold;"> 3. Set up environment variables</span><span>
</span></span><span><span>Create a </span><span class="token token code-snippet code" style="color: rgb(204, 123, 244);">`.env`</span><span> file in the </span><span class="token token code-snippet code" style="color: rgb(204, 123, 244);">`backend/`</span><span> folder:</span></span></code></pre></div></div>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-pre-wrap leading-[1.7]">GROQ_KEY=your_groq_api_key_here
SECRET_KEY=your_secret_key_here</p>

overflow: hidden; position: relative;"><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words"># 🤖 RecruitAI
An AI-powered recruitment platform built with Flask and Groq (LLaMA 3.3), designed to streamline hiring for recruiters and job searching for candidates.
## ✨ Features
### For Candidates
- 📄 **Resume Builder** — Build professional resumes with 8+ templates, live preview, and PDF/DOCX export
- 🧠 **AI Resume Parser** — Upload your existing resume (DOCX) and auto-fill the builder
- 📊 **ATS Score** — Get instant ATS compatibility score with improvement tips
- 🚀 **AI Career Tools** — Generate portfolio project ideas and career roadmaps
- 💼 **Job Board** — Browse and apply to jobs with one click
### For Recruiters
- 📋 **Job Posting** — Post and manage job listings
- 🔍 **AI Resume Screening** — Automatically screen and rank candidates
- 📊 **Analytics Dashboard** — Track applications, views, and hiring metrics
- 🤝 **Candidate Comparison** — Compare candidates side by side with AI insights
- 🎤 **AI Interview Questions** — Generate role-specific interview questions
## 🛠 Tech Stack
- **Backend** — Python, Flask, SQLite
- **AI** — Groq API (LLaMA 3.3 70B)
- **Frontend** — HTML, CSS, JavaScript
- **PDF Export** — html2pdf.js
- **DOCX Export** — docx.js
## 🚀 Getting Started
### 1. Clone the repo
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">bash
git clone https://github.com/Jyoti2103singh/RecruitAI.git
cd RecruitAI/backend
</code>
### 2. Install dependencies
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">bash
pip install -r requirements.txt
</code>
### 3. Set up environment variables
Create a <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">.env</code> file in the <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">backend/</code> folder:</p><div class="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-12 bg-gradient-to-t from-bg-300 to-transparent pointer-events-none"></div></div><button class="pb-3 pt-1 text-xs text-text-500/80 hover:text-text-100 transition w-3/4 text-left rounded-lg">Show more</button></div></div></div><div class="flex justify-start opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 group-focus-within:opacity-100 transition" role="group" aria-label="Message actions"><div class="text-text-300"><div class="text-text-300 flex items-stretch justify-between"><span class="text-text-500 text-xs flex items-center mr-2">1:48 PM</span><div class="flex items-center"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" class="cds-reset group/btn relative isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none border-0 outline-none rounded h-control font-sans text-body font-medium [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast focus-visible:shadow-focus text-primary aria-pressed:text-accent aspect-square w-control px-0 !text-muted hover:!text-primary" aria-label="Retry"><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] transition-colors duration-fast group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-expanded/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover inset-0 group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent cds-btn-squish "></span><span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" aria-hidden="true" style="font-family: var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable);
